- **Fix ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py)):** a self-serve waiter's list query is already scoped to HER (`owner OR custom_waiter`), so the branch filter is redundant AND harmful for her. `getPosInvoice` + `searchPosInvoice` now compute `is_waiter` first, resolve `branch` best-effort for a waiter (`try/except` so an unlinked waiter doesn't crash the endpoint), and **skip the `pi.branch` filter when `is_waiter`** (the per-terminal skip was already in place from round 2). Non-waiters are unchanged (branch still required + enforced).
- **Verified end-to-end on dev:** created a URY Waiter linked to a user with NO URY User row → `_get_self_waiter_for_user` resolves, `getBranch()` throws as expected, and `getPosInvoice(..., "mine")` now catches it and RETURNS her tagged draft (`WAITER SEES: 1 includes her draft: True`). Test user/waiter + the draft mutation were cleaned up/restored. `py_compile` clean. **Backend-only → `bench restart` (no migrate, no pos rebuild).**
- **Config note for the user:** the real-world trigger is the waiter's URY User being linked to a different Branch than where she rings (or not linked at all). The code now tolerates it, but it's still worth making sure her URY User branch matches her working branch for other branch-scoped features.

### 2026-07-15 (round 4) — Orders "Cashier" filter can't see waiters + sound unlock (persistent element + poll trigger)
- **Cashier filter only knew cashiers, so nobody could see a waiter's orders** — not even an admin/captain. The dropdown listed cashier users and "All Cashiers" = `owner IN (cashiers)`, which excludes waiter-placed orders (owner = the waiter). **Fix:**
  - [get_cashier_users_for_terminal](ury/ury_pos/api.py) now also returns every active waiter, tagged `kind:"waiter"` with the value `"waiter:<record>"` (cashiers stay `kind:"cashier"`, value = user id). [OrderStatusSidebar.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderStatusSidebar.tsx) renders two optgroups — **Pick a Cashier** and **Pick a Waiter** — and "All Cashiers" is relabelled **All Staff**.
  - [_resolve_orders_scope](ury/ury_pos/api.py): a `"waiter:<record>"` value (captain-only) filters `pi.custom_waiter = <record>` (shows everything she served — self-placed AND cashier-placed-for-her). "all" broadened to `(pi.owner IN (cashiers) OR pi.custom_waiter IS NOT NULL)` so waiter activity isn't hidden. Verified on dev: dropdown returns 5 cashiers + 3 waiters; waiter filter returns her 2 orders; All-Staff includes waiter-assigned orders.
- **Sound STILL dead on the Android tablet — two fixes.** (1) The real bug: `playAlertSound` did `new Audio(url).play()` — a FRESH element each time, which mobile browsers treat as NOT user-unlocked even after an earlier tap (unlock is per-element). [alert-feedback.ts](pos/src/lib/alert-feedback.ts) now keeps ONE persistent `<audio>` element, unlocks it on the first gesture (plays it at ~0 volume once), and replays that same element for every alert. (2) The alert now ALSO fires from the notification POLL (KDS-style — the KDS triggers its sound off a poll, not the socket): [Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx) `fetchNotificationCount` (now every 15s) detects a served order it hadn't seen last poll and rings, deduped with the realtime handler via a shared seen-set. So the alert survives a flaky realtime socket.
- **Phone-off / app-closed alerts are NOT covered** and can't be with in-page audio — that needs **Web Push + a service worker** (OS-level notification that rings even when the PWA is closed). Documented as the next step; not built this round (user said "leave it for now"). Offered as a follow-up.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `tsc` + `yarn build` clean; scope/dropdown tested green on dev. **Deploy:** `bench restart` + redeploy `pos/` build (no migrate).

### 2026-07-15 (round 3) — waiter dine-in menu error (blocked order creation) + real sound file + "Draft" label
- **#1 — "Please set an active menu for Restaurant X" when the waiter rings a DINE-IN order (admin was fine).** Root cause: a dine-in table order routes through [get_restaurant_and_menu_name(table)](ury/ury/doctype/ury_order/ury_order.py) (sync_order line 44), which only resolved **room-wise or `active_menu`** — NOT **order-type-wise** menus. On a restaurant configured with per-order-type menus and no default `active_menu`, it threw. Admin didn't hit it because take-away routes through `get_menu_name` (which DOES handle order-type menus). **This blocked the waiter's order creation entirely — which is also why her My Orders looked empty (no orders got created).** **Fix:** `get_restaurant_and_menu_name` now resolves with the full fallback chain **room-wise → order-type-wise (from the table's `is_take_away`) → active_menu**. Also [getRestaurantMenu](ury/ury_pos/api.py)'s `cashier` gate (which unlocks order-type/room menu resolution) now treats a self-serve waiter as a cashier (`or bool(_get_self_waiter_for_user())`) so her menu LOAD resolves the same way. Verified on dev (returns the right `(branch, menu, restaurant)` on real tables).
- **#2 — waiter wanted "Draft" not "Pending".** She read "Pending" as "Pending KOTs". Kept the all-unpaid backend bucket but the waiter sidebar now **labels it "Draft"** (`{label: "Draft", value: "Pending"}` in [order-types.ts](pos/src/data/order-types.ts)) — value stays "Pending" so it still shows EVERY unpaid draft (take-away + dine-in), which the plain "Draft" bucket hides under "Unbilled". Per-order UI (Edit button, status line) keys off `selectedOrder.status` (always "Draft"/"Paid"), so the value alias is transparent. With #1 fixed she now actually HAS orders to see here.
- **#3 — sound STILL didn't play; use the KDS's proven approach.** The Web Audio oscillator beep from the last round didn't play on the waiter's tablet. The KDS ([URYMosaic kot.vue](URYMosaic/src/components/kot.vue)) plays a real file via `new Audio(url).play()` and works. **Fix:** generated a bundled `order-ready.wav` (two-tone chime, ~0.57s) at [pos/public/sounds/](pos/public/sounds/) → served at `/assets/ury/pos/sounds/order-ready.wav` (confirmed it lands in the build). [alert-feedback.ts](pos/src/lib/alert-feedback.ts) rewritten to `new Audio(SOUND_URL).play()` (KDS pattern), unlocked on the first user gesture by playing it muted once (browsers block audio pre-interaction). Vibration unchanged (`navigator.vibrate` — Android only; iOS/iPad has NO vibration API).
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `tsc` + `yarn build` clean; WAV bundled in the build; `get_restaurant_and_menu_name` OK on dev tables. **Deploy:** `bench restart` + redeploy `pos/` build (no migrate). **Browser test:** waiter rings a dine-in table order → no menu error; it shows under her "Draft"; served → the WAV plays (after she's tapped once) + vibrates on Android.

### 2026-07-15 (follow-up) — waiter My Orders still empty + sound/vibration dead
- **After the first batch:** Account perm ✅, cashier-placed-for-waiter alerts ✅. But My Orders was STILL empty for the waiter, and no sound/vibration fired. Root causes (both explain "alerts work but My Orders doesn't" — `get_kitchen_notifications` has NO branch/terminal/status filter; `getPosInvoice` has all three):
  - **Terminal filter.** A waiter's orders span terminals — she rings some on her tablet, a cashier rings others for her on a till. `getPosInvoice`/`searchPosInvoice` filtered by the viewer's terminal, so a cashier-placed order (tagged with the till's terminal) was excluded from her tablet's view. **Fix:** skip the per-terminal filter for waiters (`if terminal and not is_waiter`). `is_waiter` = `bool(_get_self_waiter_for_user())`, computed once and passed into `_resolve_orders_scope(self_waiter=...)` to avoid a double lookup.
  - **Draft/Unbilled split.** A dine-in table order is `invoice_printed=0 + restaurant_table set` → it lands in the **"Unbilled"** bucket, not "Draft". The waiter's My Orders defaulted to "Draft" → empty for her table orders. **Fix:** new **"Pending"** status bucket = every unpaid draft (take-away AND dine-in, `docstatus=0`, not charged-to-room) with no printed/table split; the waiter sidebar now shows **Pending / Paid** ([order-types.ts](pos/src/data/order-types.ts) `WAITER_ORDER_STATUS_TYPES`) instead of Draft/Unbilled. [OrderStatusSidebar.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderStatusSidebar.tsx) snaps a waiter off the global "Draft" default onto the first status she actually has (Pending) via an effect. Backend "Pending" added to the `status_map` in both `getPosInvoice` and `searchPosInvoice`.
- **Sound was pointing at a file that doesn't exist.** [Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx) played `new Audio('/assets/frappe/sounds/notification.mp3')` — but Frappe's sounds folder has `chime/alert/chime/error…` and **NO `notification.mp3`**, so `play()` 404'd silently. **Fix:** new [alert-feedback.ts](pos/src/lib/alert-feedback.ts) generates a Web Audio "ding-ding" beep (no asset dependency, can't 404), primed on the first user gesture (browsers block audio until interaction — Footer adds a one-time `pointerdown`/`keydown` listener). `vibrateAlert()` keeps `navigator.vibrate([250,120,250])` — **works on Android; iOS Safari has NO vibration API** (silent no-op, nothing we can do on iPad). mp3 fallback to `chime.mp3` (which DOES exist) if WebAudio is unavailable.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `tsc` + `yarn build` clean; `getPosInvoice`/`searchPosInvoice` "Pending" return all unpaid drafts on dev. **Deploy:** `bench restart` + redeploy `pos/` build (no migrate this round). **Browser test (user):** waiter My Orders now defaults to **Pending** and shows her orders (self-placed + cashier-placed, any terminal, take-away + dine-in); served alert plays a beep + vibrates on an Android tablet.

### 2026-07-15 — Waiter feedback batch: Account perm, waiter order/alert scope, cart persistence, serve sound+vibration
- **Context:** first real waiter-flow testing surfaced 5 issues. All fixed this round.
- **#2 — "user does not have access to select/read this account" on order placement.** A user with ONLY a URY role (URY Waiter / pure URY Cashier) has no ERPNext Accounts/Sales role, so `sync_order` → POS Invoice validate (which reads income/tax accounts) threw a PermissionError. **Fix:** added `Account` to `READ_ONLY_DOCTYPES` in [permissions.py](ury/permissions.py) → all URY roles get read+select on Account. Verified `ensure_role_permissions` created the `URY Waiter on Account` Custom DocPerm. **Needs `bench migrate`** on live (runs `after_migrate` → `ensure_role_permissions`).
- **#3 / #5 — waiter didn't see her orders; cashier-placed-for-waiter should show for BOTH.** The Orders "My Orders" scope was `owner`-only. A waiter's self-placed order (owner = her) should show, AND an order a cashier rang on her behalf (owner = cashier, `custom_waiter` = her) should show for both the cashier (owner) and the waiter (custom_waiter). **Fix ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py) `_resolve_orders_scope`):** for a self-serve waiter, "mine" scope becomes `(pi.owner = %s OR pi.custom_waiter = %s)`. **Splice change:** all clauses now carry the `pi.` alias INSIDE (so the parenthesised OR is valid) and the 3 callers (getPosInvoice / searchPosInvoice / get_pending_kot_count) splice `scope_clause` directly instead of `pi.{scope_clause}`. Verified the OR-clause SQL returns cashier-placed-for-waiter drafts on dev.
- **#4 / #6 — served ("food ready") alerts must reach the waiter, and only the right people.** Both the realtime push ([ury_kot_display.py](ury/ury/api/ury_kot_display.py) `serve_kot`, `publish_realtime(user=invoice_owner)`) and the poll ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py) `get_kitchen_notifications`, `WHERE pi.owner = %s`) were owner-only — so a cashier-placed-for-waiter order's "ready" alert never reached the waiter. **Fix:** `serve_kot` now resolves `custom_waiter` → `URY Waiter.user` and publishes to BOTH owner and waiter user (deduped set). `get_kitchen_notifications` uses `(pi.owner = %s OR pi.custom_waiter = %s)` for a self-serve waiter (owner-only for everyone else) — so alerts stay scoped to the people who own the order, not the whole branch.
- **#1 — cart wiped on page reload.** The in-progress cart (`activeOrders` + full order context) lived only in the Zustand store, so a reload emptied it. **Fix ([pos-store.ts](pos/src/store/pos-store.ts)):** snapshot the order context to **sessionStorage** (`ury_pos_cart`) via a single store `subscribe` (can't miss a mutation), and seed the initial state from it on load. sessionStorage (not localStorage) → survives reload, auto-clears on tab/app close; also cleared on logout ([auth-api.ts](pos/src/lib/auth-api.ts)) so a shared terminal doesn't leak a cart between users, and cleared automatically when the cart empties (reset/clear/order-type change set `activeOrders: []`). Full context (table/orderId/isUpdatingOrder) is restored so a reloaded table/update order still UPDATES its draft instead of duplicating. `initializeApp` no longer clobbers a restored customer with Cash Customer.
- **#7 — sound + vibration on "food ready".** [Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx) already played `/assets/frappe/sounds/notification.mp3` on the realtime `order_served_notification`; added `navigator.vibrate([250,120,250])` (guarded — no-op on desktop/iOS, works on Android tablets/phones where waiters run the PWA). With the #4/#6 fix the realtime event now reaches the waiter, so she gets sound+vibration whether she or the cashier placed the order.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` (permissions/api/kot_display) + `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. On dev: Account perm row created; `getPosInvoice` + `get_kitchen_notifications` execute (SQL splice + f-string OK); the waiter `(owner OR custom_waiter)` clause returns cashier-placed-for-waiter drafts. **Deploy:** `bench migrate` (Account perm) + `bench restart` (whitelisted SQL changes) + redeploy the `pos/` build. **Browser test (user):** as a waiter — place an order → it shows in My Orders; add items + reload → cart persists; cashier rings an order for her → shows in her My Orders AND the cashier's; kitchen marks served → alert + sound + vibration reach the waiter (and cashier); confirm alerts don't show for unrelated users.

### 2026-07-14 — QZ print-server: KOTs now honour `qz_host` + auto-secure (tablet printing)
- **Goal (user, upgraded from "document only"):** make tablet printing actually work — a cashier on a tablet (no local QZ Tray) prints to a networked printer via a LAN box running QZ Tray as a "print server".
- **What already existed:** the POS Profile **`qz_host`** custom field (Data, default `localhost`, all three sources + a `backfill_qz_host` patch) and the **bill** print path ([print.ts](pos/src/lib/print.ts) → `printWithQz(qzHost, ...)`) already read it. So bill printing to a remote gateway was half-wired.
- **The two gaps that broke tablet printing:**
  1. **KOTs ignored `qz_host`.** [print-qz.ts](pos/src/lib/print-qz.ts) `printKotWithQz` hardcoded `const host = 'localhost'`, and [kot-listener.ts](pos/src/lib/kot-listener.ts) never had the host to pass. So on a tablet the bill went to the gateway but every KOT tried `localhost` and failed (no local QZ).
  2. **`usingSecure` was hardcoded `false`.** From an HTTPS POS page, `ws://<remote-ip>` is **mixed content and browsers silently block it** — so even the bill path was actually broken to a remote host on HTTPS. Only `wss://` connects. (localhost is exempt — loopback is treated as trustworthy.)
- **Fix — backend ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py)):** `get_latest_kot` and `print_pending_kots_for_invoice` now resolve the profile's `qz_host` (empty ⇒ `localhost`) and return it on every response that carries print jobs/printers (PU-mode, Menu-Course, and legacy paths). No schema change — `qz_host` already exists; just returning it in two more endpoints. **Needs `bench restart`** (whitelisted return-shape change), no migrate.
- **Fix — frontend:**
  - [print-qz.ts](pos/src/lib/print-qz.ts): new `qzUsesSecure(host)` — `localhost`/`127.0.0.1`/empty → insecure WS (loopback exempt), any remote host → **WSS (secure, 8181)**. `loadQzPrinter` uses it instead of the hardcoded `usingSecure:false`. `printKotWithQz(printer, html, host='localhost')` now takes a host.
  - [kot-listener.ts](pos/src/lib/kot-listener.ts): destructures `qz_host` from both endpoints' responses and threads it into all three `printKotWithQz` call sites (auto-fire `print_jobs`, legacy `printers`, and `firePendingKotsForInvoice`).
- **Config to tell a customer:** set **POS Profile → QZ Host** to the gateway PC's LAN IP; run QZ Tray on that PC with the printer(s) attached; trust QZ's cert on each tablet (iOS config profile / Android trust store) since a remote host uses WSS. Description on the field updated (setup.py + custom_field.json) to explain this. See the **Gotchas** QZ entry for the full cert/mixed-content detail.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `json.tool` + `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. `bench execute get_latest_kot` on the dev site (PU mode) returned `"qz_host": "localhost"` in the print-jobs payload — chain confirmed end-to-end. Full remote-gateway + tablet run is the user's to walk (needs real hardware). **Deploy:** `bench restart` + redeploy the `pos/` build; description update lands on `bench migrate`.
- **Known trade-off:** an HTTP-served POS wanting insecure `ws://` to a REMOTE gateway is no longer supported (auto-derive forces WSS for remote). If a deployment needs that, add an explicit `custom_qz_secure` toggle. The tablet case is HTTPS → WSS, which is correct.

### 2026-07-14 — Simplified waiter POS UI (frontend-only) + QZ tablet-printing research (doc-only)
- **Goal (user):** when a waiter-only user logs in on mobile/tablet, de-crowd the POS. A waiter PLACES ORDERS ONLY — she sees her own orders (paid/pending), tables (occupied/available), and rings orders on tables. She does NO payments, invoicing, printing, or shift close (she hands off to the cashier). Confirmed decisions: she may **edit her own Drafts** (view-only once billed/paid); nav = **POS · Tables · My Orders · Alerts** (Alerts kept so she sees kitchen "served"); Reports + Waiters hidden.
- **The gate:** new [`isWaiterOnly(user)`](pos/src/lib/role-utils.ts) — has the **URY Waiter** role AND is not elevated (Administrator / System Manager / URY Manager / URY Captain). Mirrors the backend self-waiter gate. A user who is ALSO a cashier is NOT a waiter-only user (don't give the waiter role to a cashier).
- **Changes (all `pos/` frontend, no backend/migrate):**
  - **Nav ([Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx)):** waiter gets a 4-tab nav (POS / Tables / My Orders / Alerts); everyone else keeps the full cashier nav (+ Waiters when `custom_use_waiter`, + Reports).
  - **Orders action bar ([Orders.tsx](pos/src/pages/Orders.tsx)):** for a waiter the sticky Payment/Print/Reprint/Return/Merge bar is replaced by a read-only line — a **Paid**/**Pending — see the cashier to bill** status + the order total. The header **Edit** button stays but is restricted to **Draft only** for waiters (was Draft/Unbilled/Recently Paid); Cancel already hidden (captain-only). The top **Merge Orders** button is already hidden (waiter fails `canMergeOrders`).
  - **Orders sidebar ([order-types.ts](pos/src/data/order-types.ts) `getOrderStatusTypes(..., waiterMode)` + [OrderStatusSidebar.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderStatusSidebar.tsx)):** waiter sees only **Draft / Unbilled / Paid** (+ Recently Paid when enabled) — not Pending KOTs / Cancelled / Incoming Transfers / Room Charges / Consolidated. Cashier filter already hidden (fails `canSeeAllTerminalOrders`).
  - **Header ([Header.tsx](pos/src/components/Header.tsx)):** **End Shift** hidden for waiters (the cashier opens/closes the day). Change Terminal / Switch to Desk / Enroll Biometrics already elevated-only.
  - **Route guard ([RequireNotWaiter.tsx](pos/src/components/RequireNotWaiter.tsx), wired in [App.tsx](pos/src/App.tsx)):** a waiter who manually types `/reports` or `/waiters` is bounced to `/orders`. Done as a route wrapper (NOT an in-page early return) because Reports.tsx has many hooks after the role read — an early return there would break the Rules of Hooks.
  - **POS page:** no change needed — [OrderPanel.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderPanel.tsx) already has no payment path (it only rings drafts) and already auto-assigns `self_waiter`. Her Orders list is scoped to her own (`owner` = her user; backend `_resolve_orders_scope` downgrades non-elevated users to "mine").
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean + `yarn build` clean (bundle 973 kB; dynamic-import + chunk-size warnings are pre-existing). Frontend-only → **no `bench migrate`**; deploy = redeploy the `pos/` build. **Still to browser-test (user):** log in as a waiter-only user on a tablet → 4-tab nav; ring an order on a table; open it in My Orders → can Edit the Draft, no Payment/Print bar, status line shows Pending + total; a paid order is view-only; sidebar shows only Draft/Unbilled/Paid; user menu has no End Shift; typing `/reports` bounces to `/orders`. Log in as a cashier → everything unchanged.
- **QZ tablet-printing research (doc-only, user chose "document only for now"):** written up in the **Gotchas** section above — QZ Tray can't run on iOS/Android (no JVM), so tablet printing needs a LAN box running QZ as a "print server" and a per-profile QZ **host** setting in `print-qz.ts` (currently hardcoded `localhost`), plus trusting the QZ cert on each tablet (or insecure-WS + a Chrome flag). No printing code changed this round.

### 2026-07-14 — Self-serve waiter (waiter logs in + auto-assigns herself) + URY branding cleanup
- **Feature:** a waiter can log in and ring HER OWN orders — auto-assigned to herself, no picker. Cashiers/captains/managers/admins still pick the waiter (when `custom_use_waiter` is on).
- **Schema/roles:** new `user` (Link → User, unique) field on **URY Waiter** (maps a login user to a waiter); new **"URY Waiter" role** in [role.json](ury/fixtures/role.json) + auto-created on migrate via [permissions.py](ury/permissions.py) `_ensure_role_exists`. URY Waiter added to the POS permission baseline `ROLES` (same as cashier — create POS Invoice etc.), to the URY Waiter doctype read/select perm, to `URY_LOGIN_ROLES`/`URY_LOGIN_SEARCH_ROLES` ([biometric/api.py](ury/ury/biometric/api.py)), and to the desk-redirect ([cashier_desk_redirect.js](ury/public/js/cashier_desk_redirect.js)) so waiters land on /pos.
- **Resolver [`_get_self_waiter_for_user`](ury/ury_pos/api.py):** returns her `{name, full_name, mobile_number}` ONLY if she has the URY Waiter role, is linked (`URY Waiter.user`), and is NOT elevated (admin/System Manager/URY Manager/URY Captain) — else None (they pick). Exposed via `getPosProfile` as `self_waiter`; **enforced in [sync_order](ury/ury/doctype/ury_order/ury_order.py)** (forces her waiter server-side, can't be spoofed).
- **Frontend:** [OrderPanel.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderPanel.tsx) auto-uses `posProfile.self_waiter` (picker never opens) + shows a "Serving as {name}" badge; `self_waiter` threaded through [pos-profile-api.ts](pos/src/lib/pos-profile-api.ts).
- **Branding cleanup (same commit):** role translations added to [en.csv](ury/translations/en.csv) (`URY Cashier/Captain/Manager → ExPOS ...`; Waiter already there). POS Profile field **descriptions + labels** cleaned of "URY" → "ExPOS" (18 in custom_field.json + 14 in setup.py, scoped to POS Profile fields only via a one-off format-preserving script; **preserved** `URYMosaic` literal + all `options`/`depends_on` doctype-link strings).
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + JSON valid + `tsc` + `yarn build` clean; `bench migrate` created the `user` column + URY Waiter role; resolver link-lookup finds a linked waiter + negative gating (admin/non-waiter → None) confirmed. (Positive path relies on standard `frappe.get_roles` on a fresh login — the mid-request role-add in the throwaway test hit a role cache, not a code bug.) **Config:** assign the URY Waiter role to the waiter's User + set the URY Waiter's `user`; add URY Waiter to the POS Profile's Role Allowed For Billing if it's restricted. **Needs `bench migrate` + `bench restart`** on live + redeploy the `pos/` build.
- **Infra note:** the local dev bench's `redis_cache` was flaky (full `bench start` took too long / `bench migrate` refused with "redis not running" or timed out). Workaround that finally worked: start just the redis servers daemonized — `redis-server config/redis_cache.conf --daemonize yes` + `redis_queue.conf` — then `bench migrate`. They persist across tool calls (unlike a backgrounded `bench start`, which gets SIGTERM'd on command teardown).

### 2026-07-13 — Order save crashed: `Unknown column 'tabContact.is_billing_contact'`
- **Symptom:** every order (`sync_order` → `invoice.save()`) 500'd with `MySQLdb.OperationalError (1054) Unknown column 'tabContact.is_billing_contact' in 'WHERE'`. Traceback ran through POS Invoice validate → `set_missing_lead_customer_details` → `_get_party_details` → `set_contact_details` → ERPNext's `get_default_contact`.
- **Root cause (NOT in ExPOS).** `grep -r is_billing_contact apps/ury` → **zero matches**. The reference is in **ERPNext core** [party.py `get_default_contact`](apps/erpnext/erpnext/accounts/party.py#L1027) — it filters (`or_filters`) and `order_by`s on `Contact.is_billing_contact`, but this Frappe version dropped that column from the Contact doctype. Classic ERPNext↔Frappe version drift: ERPNext still queries a column Frappe removed. The user *thought* it was an ExPOS reference to deprecate — it isn't; we can't remove it without patching the ERPNext dependency.
- **Fix (URY-owned, survives updates):** re-add `is_billing_contact` as a **hidden, read-only, no_copy Check (default 0)** custom field on **Contact**, so ERPNext's query finds the column again. All three sources (custom_field.json + [setup.py](ury/setup.py) `get_custom_fields()` new "Contact" key + [hooks.py](ury/hooks.py) fixtures `Contact-is_billing_contact`). `insert_after: is_primary_contact`. It's an inert no-op (always 0) — the column existing is the entire fix; a site that later gets a real `is_billing_contact` coexists fine.
- **Why not monkeypatch `get_default_contact`?** Fragile (no clean boot hook, would shadow future ERPNext fixes). Re-adding the column is the standard, robust workaround for "dependency queries a column another app removed."
- **Verified on dev:** `bench migrate` created the column (`frappe.db.has_column("Contact","is_billing_contact") == True`); `erpnext.accounts.party.get_default_contact("Customer","Ernest")` now returns `None` cleanly (no exception). `py_compile` + `json.tool` clean. Backend-only — **needs `bench migrate`** on live (then the order flow works). No `bench restart` strictly needed (schema only), but harmless.

### 2026-07-13 — POS mobile/tablet polish + installable PWA
- **Context:** follow-up to the mobile/tablet responsive revamp (commit `756ad6c`). User feedback: menu cards too big (only 2 per row), the bottom nav + cart bar getting cut off, and wanted a floating cart icon instead of the full-width bar — plus make the POS an installable PWA that prompts to install on mobile/tablet.
- **Smaller cards / more per row.** [MenuCard.tsx](pos/src/components/MenuCard.tsx) is now compact + responsive (`h-40 sm:h-52 xl:h-56`, `h-16 sm:h-24` image, smaller text, course line `hidden sm:block`). [MenuList.tsx](pos/src/components/MenuList.tsx) grid → `grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-2 sm:gap-3` (3 on phones, 4 on tablets).
- **Bottom nav + cart cut off = the mobile `100vh` bug** (browser address bar eats the bottom). [App.tsx](pos/src/App.tsx) app shell switched from `h-screen` → **`h-[100dvh]`** (dynamic viewport height, Tailwind 3.4). Footer + FAB now stay visible.
- **Cart bar → floating cart FAB.** [POS.tsx](pos/src/pages/POS.tsx): removed the full-width `< lg` cart bar; added a round `fixed bottom-20 right-4` cart button with a red **item-count badge** that opens the cart drawer (hidden while the drawer is open). (The drawer + category chips + `< lg`/`lg`/`xl` breakpoints are from `756ad6c`.)
- **Installable PWA.** New [manifest.json](pos/public/manifest.json) (`start_url`/`scope` = `/pos`, `display: standalone`, theme `#2563eb`) + icons in [pos/public/icons/](pos/public/icons/) (192 / 512 / maskable-512 / apple-touch-180, generated from `ury_pos.png` via Pillow in the **bench env python** — system python had no PIL). [index.html](pos/index.html) got the manifest link + theme-color + apple/mobile meta + `viewport-fit=cover`. **Used `manifest.json` (not `.webmanifest`)** so Frappe/nginx serve it with a JSON content-type (`.webmanifest` MIME isn't reliably registered). Vite `--base=/assets/ury/pos/` → public files land at `/assets/ury/pos/`, so all PWA URLs are absolute `/assets/ury/pos/...`.
- **Smart install prompt.** New [InstallPrompt.tsx](pos/src/components/InstallPrompt.tsx) (mounted in App.tsx): shows ONLY on mobile/tablet, only if not already `display-mode: standalone`, once (dismissal in localStorage `ury_pwa_install_dismissed`). Android/Chromium captures `beforeinstallprompt` → one-tap Install; iOS Safari (no bip) shows an "Add to Home Screen" hint after ~1.8s.
- **No service worker (yet).** Modern Chrome (108+) doesn't need one for installability; doing it cleanly needs a Frappe route so the SW scope can cover `/pos` (assets are under `/assets/ury/pos/`). Offline caching is a future add-on.
- **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean; manifest.json valid + lands in the build output + linked in the built `pos.html`; icons served under `/assets/ury/pos/icons/`. **PWA install needs HTTPS** → test on the live site on a real tablet/phone (can't verify headlessly). Frontend-only — no backend/migrate; redeploy the `pos/` build.

### 2026-06-15 — Configurable min screen width (POS Profile `custom_min_screen_width`)
- **Goal:** stop hardcoding the "Desktop Only" cutoff (was 1024, then 1000). New POS Profile Int field **`custom_min_screen_width`** — blank/0 = **no restriction** (POS works at any size); a positive value blocks the POS below that px width. Field in all three sources (custom_field.json + setup.py + hooks.py) with a description that also warns the value is the browser VIEWPORT width (shrinks with zoom / non-maximized window — which is why "Current screen width" changed from 1020→931 on the same screen: the user zoomed in).
- **The wiring wrinkle:** [ScreenSizeProvider.tsx](pos/src/components/ScreenSizeProvider.tsx) wraps AuthGuard + the whole app, so it runs BEFORE the POS Profile is ever fetched — it can't read the store. Solution: [getPosProfileLimitedFields](pos/src/lib/pos-profile-api.ts) (the base profile fetch every path funnels through) writes `custom_min_screen_width` to `localStorage['ury_min_screen_width']`; ScreenSizeProvider reads that on mount + resize (+ a cross-tab `storage` listener). 0/absent/invalid → no gate. A changed value applies on the NEXT load (one-load lag — acceptable; config changes are rare, and the gate never blocks a first-ever load because the key is absent → 0). [getPosProfile](ury/ury_pos/api.py) returns the field; [ScreenSizeDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/ScreenSizeDialog.tsx) takes a `requiredWidth` prop + shows a "reset zoom (Ctrl/⌘+0) & maximize" tip.
- **Verified:** `bench migrate` (88 fields), getPosProfile returns 1000 when set / 0 when blank, `tsc` + `yarn build` clean. **Needs `bench restart`.** After deploy the currently-blocked user gets in immediately (the localStorage key is absent → no restriction) unless an admin sets a positive width.

### 2026-06-14 — "Add to ExPOS Menu" button on the Item form (with confirm-on-change)
- **Feature (user spec):** a button on the **Item** desk form, "Add to ExPOS Menu", that opens a dialog to pick the Menu + Course + Rate, and on **Create** appends the item to that URY Menu and regenerates the menu's price list. **No Price List field** — the rate goes straight into the selected menu's own price list. **If the item is already on the menu**, it's not changed silently: the user is told it's already there, and if the rate or course differs, the exact changes are shown and must be confirmed before applying.
- **Backend ([ury/ury/doctype/ury_menu/ury_menu.py](ury/ury/doctype/ury_menu/ury_menu.py)):**
  - `add_item_to_menu(item, menu, course=None, rate=None, confirm_update=0)` (whitelisted). Not-on-menu → append a URY Menu Item row + `menu_doc.save()` (→ `on_update → make_price_list()`, so it's priced in the menu's own price list with `uom = stock_uom` per the 2026-04-08 fix) → `{"action": "added"}`. Already-on-menu → diff rate/course: no diff → `{"action": "unchanged"}`; diff + no confirm → `{"action": "needs_confirmation", "changes": [...]}`; diff + `confirm_update=1` → update the row + save → `{"action": "updated", "changes": [...]}`.
  - `get_menu_item_details(menu, item)` (whitelisted) — returns `{exists, rate, course}` so the dialog pre-fills the current values when a menu is picked.
  - Three pure helpers (unit-tested): `_pick_menu_item_rate(explicit, standard)` (explicit > standard > 0; `None`/`""` unset, explicit `0` wins), `_find_menu_item_row(items, item)`, `_diff_menu_item(existing_rate, existing_course, new_rate, new_course)` (uses `flt()` so `80` vs `80.0` isn't a change; treats course `None` ≡ `""`).
- **Frontend (desk):** [ury/public/js/item_add_to_menu.js](ury/public/js/item_add_to_menu.js) — button (skipped on `is_new()`); dialog = Menu (Link URY Menu, reqd) / Course (Link URY Menu Course) / Rate (Currency) / status HTML. Menu `onchange` calls `get_menu_item_details` → pre-fills rate+course and shows "already on this menu — edit to update". Create calls `add_item_to_menu`; on `needs_confirmation` it pops a `frappe.confirm` with a Field/Current/New table and re-calls with `confirm_update=1` on yes. `added`/`updated` → green alert; `unchanged` → blue alert. Wired via `doctype_js["Item"]` in [ury/hooks.py](ury/hooks.py). Label is "Add to ExPOS Menu" (brand layer); DocType stays `URY Menu`.
- **Branch:** moved ury from `ury_pos_updates` (2 commits ahead + 1 uncommitted `pos/src/lib/pos-display.ts`, stashed as `stash@{0}`) onto `upstream/version-16` (fast-forwarded +425 commits to `7360656`) before building, per the user's request.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `node -c` clean; **16/16 unit tests pass** via `run_menu_tests` (rate precedence, row lookup, and diff incl. float-equality / course-None≡"" / set-to-blank) — run with `env/bin/python -m unittest` because Redis/bench wasn't up. Asset live via the `sites/assets/ury → apps/ury/ury/public` symlink (no `bench build`). **Needs `bench start`/restart** so the new whitelisted methods load + the `doctype_js` hook is picked up (if the button doesn't appear: `bench --site 16.local clear-cache` + hard-refresh). Browser-test: Item → Add to ExPOS Menu → new item adds; re-open same item+menu, change the rate → confirm dialog shows "Rate: old → new"; no change → "already on menu — no changes".
### 2026-06-12 — Merge button Draft-only + Waiters page drag-and-drop reassignment
- **Merge Orders button only on the Draft filter.** [Orders.tsx](pos/src/pages/Orders.tsx): the Merge Orders button gate changed from `canMerge && selectedStatus !== 'Pending KOTs'` to `canMerge && selectedStatus === 'Draft'` — it no longer shows on Cancelled / Paid / Unbilled / Recently Paid / etc. The "exit merge mode on filter change" effect now fires for any `selectedStatus !== 'Draft'` (was just Pending KOTs).
- **Waiters page: drag-and-drop to reassign an order's waiter.** Cashier picked the wrong waiter → drag the order card onto the correct waiter. Native HTML5 DnD (desktop POS, mouse). [Waiters.tsx](pos/src/pages/Waiters.tsx) rewritten: order cards are `draggable` (grip affordance, `cursor-grab`, opacity/scale while dragging); waiter cards are drop zones (dashed blue outline when a drag is active, strong ring + "Drop to move order to X" banner + scale when hovered; empty waiters show a "Drop an order here" zone). Optimistic move + green flash on success, **revert on failure**. Backend: new whitelisted `reassign_order_waiter(invoice, waiter)` in [ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py) — stamps `POS Invoice.custom_waiter`; DRAFT-only; a plain cashier can only move their OWN orders (captain+ any). `get_waiters_with_pending_orders` gained an `include_empty` param so the page shows EVERY active waiter as a drop target (default 0 keeps the old "only waiters with orders" behavior); [waiter-api.ts](pos/src/lib/waiter-api.ts) `getWaitersWithPendingOrders(includeEmpty)` + new `reassignOrderWaiter`.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. Backend: reassign moves the waiter (sarah→Afia→reverted), paid invoice rejected with a clear error, `include_empty=1` returns all active waiters. **Needs `bench restart`** (new whitelisted method + signature change). Browser-test the drag UX (can't be exercised headlessly).

### 2026-06-12 — KOT Thermal Print: qty wasn't showing + bold everything
- **Qty rendered as `0x`.** [kot_thermal_print.json](ury/ury/print_format/kot_thermal_print/kot_thermal_print.json) item loop used `{{ item.qty|int }}`, but the URY KOT Items field is **`quantity`** (Data), so `item.qty` was always None → `None|int` = 0 → every item showed `0x`. Fixed to `{{ item.quantity }}` (raw value — robust to decimals; `|int` would also break on a `"2.0"` string). Verified `quantity` holds clean strings like `'1'`.
- **Bold everything.** Added `font-weight: bold !important` to the existing `*` rule (single-line edit) so the whole ticket prints bold.
- **THE GOTCHA — standard print formats need a bumped `modified` to re-import.** `KOT Thermal Print` is a repo file (`standard: Yes`), synced to the DB on `bench migrate`. Frappe SKIPS re-importing a standard record when the file's `modified` timestamp isn't newer than the DB record's — so editing the HTML alone is a silent no-op on migrate (confirmed: first migrate didn't update the DB; render still showed `0x`/no bold). **Fix:** bump `"modified"` in the JSON (set to `2026-06-12 10:00:00.000000`). After that, migrate re-imported it and the render showed `1x` + bold. Always bump `modified` when editing a standard print format / report JSON in the repo, or the change never reaches any site.
- **Verified:** `json.tool` clean; `bench migrate` re-imported (DB modified = 2026-06-12); render-test on a real KOT shows `1x <item>` (no `0x`) and the bold CSS present. Repo change → deploys via `bench migrate` on live (no rebuild). Carbonite POS (the sibling task) is a customer-specific DB format, edited in the desk, NOT in the repo.

### 2026-06-12 — Auto-print bill on payment + Mosaic KDS timer (timezone-safe)
- **Auto-print bill on payment.** [PaymentDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/PaymentDialog.tsx) `handlePayment` only printed receipts for value-split; a normal (single-payer) payment printed nothing. Added an `else if (storePosProfile)` branch after `make_invoice` succeeds that calls `printOrder({ orderId: invoice, posProfile: storePosProfile })`. A print failure is caught and toasted ("Bill not printed — reprint from the Orders page") so it can't undo the already-submitted payment. **Note:** the Orders page still gates Payment behind a first bill print (`invoice_printed`), so this is a second (paid) print — flag to the user if they'd rather skip the pre-payment print.
- **Mosaic KDS timer stuck at 00:00 (timezone/clock dependent).** [kot.vue](URYMosaic/src/components/kot.vue) `_elapsedSeconds` parsed Frappe's site-local `creation` string as **browser-local** time (`new Date(iso)`) then did `Date.now() - target`. If the KDS device's timezone OR clock was ahead of the server, `diff` went negative and the `diff < 0` guard returned 0 — the timer never moved. (Dev server tz is `Africa/Accra`; on a same-tz/clock device the old path happened to work, which is why it wasn't caught.) **Fix:** the backend ([kot_list](ury/ury/api/ury_kot_display.py)) now sends a server-computed `elapsed_seconds` per KOT (both sides in system tz → tz-safe); the client ticks forward from that base using only the LOCAL delta `(Date.now() - kot._fetchedAt)`, which is correct regardless of the device's absolute clock or timezone. `_fetchedAt` is stamped when KOTs arrive (in `fetchKOT` and on the realtime `unshift`; realtime KOTs are new so base defaults to 0). Legacy creation-parsing kept as a fallback. **Both frontends rebuilt** (`pos` + `URYMosaic`); **needs `bench restart`** (kot_list is whitelisted). Deploy note: the Mosaic build must be re-deployed (`yarn build` in URYMosaic/ → `ury/public/URYMosaic`).

### 2026-06-11 — Per-item warehouse mode (POS Profile "Use Single POS Warehouse" toggle)
- **Analysis:** POS Profile.warehouse (reqd) flows into POS Invoice `set_warehouse` ([pos_invoice.py set_pos_fields](apps/erpnext/erpnext/accounts/doctype/pos_invoice/pos_invoice.py#L691)), which fills every item row's warehouse — and stock is deferred to consolidation at shift close. So everything posts to the one profile warehouse.
- **Feature (user spec, confirmed):** new POS Profile checkbox **`custom_use_pos_warehouse`** (default ON = current behavior). When OFF, the Warehouse field hides + isn't required and each item posts stock to its **own Default Warehouse** (Item Defaults for the company) at close; an item with no warehouse blocks the close with a clear, item-named error so a manager fixes it.
- **Schema:** `custom_use_pos_warehouse` in all three sources (custom_field.json + setup.py + hooks.py). The standard `warehouse` field is made conditional via **3 Property Setters** created idempotently in [install.py `_ensure_pos_warehouse_optional`](ury/install.py) (after_install + after_migrate): `reqd→0`, `mandatory_depends_on`/`depends_on = eval:doc.custom_use_pos_warehouse`.
- **Order time ([sync_order](ury/ury/doctype/ury_order/ury_order.py)):** when the box is OFF, each appended item row gets `warehouse = _resolve_item_warehouse(item, company)` (Item Default's default_warehouse) and `invoice.set_warehouse = None` so ERPNext doesn't force one. [ury_pos_profile.py validate](ury/ury/hooks/ury_pos_profile.py) `clear_warehouse_in_item_mode` blanks the profile warehouse when the box is OFF so `set_pos_fields` can't re-force it. **Verified ERPNext keeps the explicit per-item warehouse through `set_missing_values`** (item kept its warehouse even with a different set_warehouse).
- **Close pre-flight ([_validate_item_warehouses](ury/ury_pos/api.py)):** called from `submit_pos_closing_entry` right after `_validate_close_preflight`. In item mode, for every distinct STOCK item sold this shift, checks the Item has a Default Warehouse for the company; if any don't, `frappe.throw` names them with a "set a Default Warehouse, then reopen the close dialog" message (surfaces in the close dialog's error banner). No-op in single-warehouse mode and when the field is unset (default ON). Verified: caught "1 Liter Fanta" (no default warehouse) with the clear error; POS-warehouse mode = no-op.
- **Verified:** `bench migrate` clean (87 fields; property setters created), `py_compile` clean. No frontend change (POS Profile desk setting; the close error uses the existing close-dialog error pipeline). **Needs `bench restart`.** Still to verify in browser (user): flip the box OFF on a profile, ring an order → items post to their own warehouses; close with a misconfigured item → the clear error blocks until the manager sets the item's Default Warehouse.

### 2026-06-11 — Cancelled orders filter + cashier cancel/edit restrictions (3 features)
- **Feature 1 — "Cancelled" Orders filter + reasons.** New "Cancelled" entry in `BASE_ORDER_STATUS_TYPES` ([order-types.ts](pos/src/data/order-types.ts)) + the `OrderStatusType` union. Backend: the `getPosInvoice` status_map default already maps an unknown status to `pi.status = <status>`, so "Cancelled" → `pi.status = 'Cancelled'` (docstatus=2) with no new branch; added `pi.cancel_reason` to the SELECT. `cancel_reason` is set by `cancel_order` and already a column. Frontend: `cancel_reason` on the POSInvoice types ([invoice-api.ts](pos/src/lib/invoice-api.ts) + [orders-slice.ts](pos/src/store/slices/orders-slice.ts)); [Orders.tsx](pos/src/pages/Orders.tsx) right panel shows a red "Order Cancelled — Reason: …" banner for cancelled orders. (Pick the order's date in the sidebar — cancelled orders honor the posting-date filter.)
- **Feature 2 — only captains can cancel.** New `isCaptainOrAbove(user)` in [role-utils.ts](pos/src/lib/role-utils.ts) (Administrator / System Manager / URY Manager / URY Captain). The Cancel (X) button in the Orders right-panel header is hidden for cashiers (`canCancel = isCaptainOrAbove(user)`). Backend defense-in-depth: [cancel_order](ury/ury/doctype/ury_order/ury_order.py) now throws "Only a captain or manager can cancel an order" for non-captains.
- **Feature 3 — cashiers can't reduce/remove already-ordered items (the "empty the order to delete it" loophole).** When an EXISTING order is opened for edit, the store snapshots each cart line's original qty into `lockedItemQtys` (keyed by uniqueId) — set in `loadTableOrder` and via a new `snapshotLockedQtys()` action called by Orders.tsx `handleEditOrder` after hydrating; cleared in `resetOrderState` / `setOrderForUpdate(null)`. In [OrderPanel.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderPanel.tsx), `restrictExistingItems = isUpdatingOrder && !isCaptainOrAbove(user)`; for a locked original line the cashier can only INCREASE — the decrease (−), remove (trash), and per-item Edit (product dialog) are disabled/hidden, and the whole-cart "Clear cart" button is hidden. New items added this session stay fully editable (their uniqueId isn't in `lockedItemQtys`). Captains keep full control. `addToOrder` regenerates uniqueId deterministically, so adding more of an original item merges into its line (just increases qty) rather than creating an unlocked one.
- **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean; `cancel_reason` column confirmed; backend `py_compile` clean. No new custom field (cancel_reason pre-exists) → no migrate, but **needs `bench restart`** for the `cancel_order` guard. Note: Feature 3 is UI-enforced (matches the user's "restrict in the POS" framing); a hacked client could still POST a reduced item list — a sync_order backend guard is a possible follow-up.

### 2026-06-11 — URY Production Unit KOT printing: order-time fallback + Pending-KOTs fire/mark
- **Symptom:** in URY Production Unit (ExPOS Production) KDS mode — (1) a KOT for an item whose category isn't matched to a production didn't print at order time (only the bill); (2) printing from Orders → "Pending KOTs" printed the BILL format instead of the KOT, and didn't mark the KOT printed (so it never left the pending list).
- **Diagnosis (dev data):** profile in PU + QZ mode; KOTs were being created with `production=None` because the ordered item's `item_group` (e.g. a drink's "NON ALCOHOLIC") wasn't in any production's `URY Production Item Groups` (the bar unit was unconfigured). PU mode had **no print path for production-less KOTs**, and `print_pending_kots_for_invoice` **short-circuited to an empty list** in PU mode.
- **Fixes ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py)):**
  - New shared helper `_build_pu_print_jobs_for_kot(kot_doc)` — renders the KOT to each of its production's `URY Printer Settings` rows (`custom_kot_print=1`) using the row's KOT print format. Used by both order-time + pending paths.
  - `get_latest_kot` (order-time): the PU branch now only fires for KOTs that actually have a `production`; a `production=None` fallback KOT **falls through to the Menu Course department routing** (`resolve_kot_print_plan`) instead of dead-ending — so unmatched items still print where department printers are configured.
  - `print_pending_kots_for_invoice`: removed the PU short-circuit. In PU mode it now builds print jobs for every un-printed KOT (production printers for matched KOTs, department routing for production-less ones) and returns `production_unit_mode=1`.
  - Frontend ([kot-listener.ts](pos/src/lib/kot-listener.ts)): `firePendingKotsForInvoice` reads `production_unit_mode` and marks the **whole KOT** printed (`mark_kot_printed`) after firing in PU mode (vs per-department in Menu Course) — so the KOT leaves the Pending list.
- **Verified:** a matched KOT → 1 job to "Kitchen Order Printer" with rendered HTML; the pending endpoint returns `production_unit_mode=1` with jobs even for the production-less drink (department fallback). `py_compile` + `tsc` + `yarn build` clean. **Needs `bench restart`.**
- **Config note (told the user):** for drinks to route to the BAR rather than fall back, add the drink item groups + a printer (with a KOT print format) to the bar's URY Production Unit; and set a KOT print format on each production's printer row (CXCKitchen's was blank → renders the default format).
- **Follow-up (same day) — exact-spec rework + the REAL order-time root cause.** User's precise spec: at order time print the KOT only if an item matches a production (else nothing); from Pending KOTs fire ONE KOT (KOT format, the **terminal's** printer), mark it, no bill, no default-format fallback. **Root cause of order-time non-printing found:** in PU mode `process_items_for_kot` created the fallback KOT (`production=None`) LAST, so it was the "latest" KOT that `get_latest_kot` returns → it masked the real production KOT, which then never auto-printed; and it was the stray default-format ticket in Pending KOTs. **Changes:** (1) [process_items_for_kot](ury/ury/api/ury_kot_generate.py) no longer creates the fallback KOT in PU mode (`unmatched_items and kds_mode != "URY Production Unit"`) — unmatched items get no ticket. (2) [get_latest_kot](ury/ury_pos/api.py): PU branch prints ONLY production-matched KOTs and does NOT fall back to department routing; a production-less KOT prints nothing. (3) [print_pending_kots_for_invoice](ury/ury_pos/api.py): PU mode builds jobs only for matched KOTs (no department fallback), and marks any legacy `production=None` KOT printed so it leaves the list. (4) **Per-terminal printer routing** — new `custom_terminal` (Link → URY POS Terminal) on **URY Printer Settings** (custom_field.json + setup.py + hooks.py); `_build_pu_print_jobs_for_kot(kot_doc, terminal)` keeps only printer rows tagged for that terminal (+ untagged rows, which print everywhere); terminal derived from the KOT's invoice `custom_terminal`. (5) Frontend: new `handleFirePendingKot` in [Orders.tsx](pos/src/pages/Orders.tsx) fires the KOT only (NO bill) and the Pending-KOTs Print button uses it. **Verified:** `bench migrate` (86 fields), `custom_terminal` created, matched KOT renders to its printer(s) (CXCKitchen has 2 untagged rows → 2 jobs until the user tags each with a terminal). `py_compile` + `tsc` + `yarn build` clean. **Needs `bench restart`.** **Config to tell the user:** set `Terminal` on each of a production's printer rows so only that terminal's printer fires (CXCKitchen has 2 untagged printers → both currently fire).

### 2026-06-11 — Shift close: surface the real error (force sync consolidation + stronger Mode-of-Payment pre-flight)
- **Symptom:** user added more Modes of Payment (was cash-only) and the shift won't close — **with no error message** (and, after the force-sync below, the masked error `Could not find Reference Name: POS-CLO-…`).
- **Update (same day) — unmask the real error.** After the force-sync (below) made the failure synchronous, the user saw `Could not find Reference Name: POS-CLO-2026-00013`. That's the MASK: ERPNext's `create_merge_logs` catches the genuine error, computes its message via `get_error_message()`, then calls `closing_entry.set_status("Failed")` — but the entry was just `frappe.db.rollback()`-ed, so set_status raises "Could not find Reference Name" and hides the cause. **Fix:** `submit_pos_closing_entry` now also monkeypatches `pos_invoice_merge_log.get_error_message` to capture the real message; in the `except`, when the visible error is the "could not find reference name" mask, it re-throws the **captured** real consolidation error (and logs it to the Error Log "URY: shift close failed — real cause (unmasked)"). So the cashier/desk now sees the genuine cause (valuation rate / order type / customer / MoP account / etc.).
- **Root-cause analysis (two failure paths):** (1) ERPNext **enqueues** invoice consolidation in a background worker when a shift has **≥ 10 invoices** ([pos_invoice_merge_log.consolidate_pos_invoices](apps/erpnext/erpnext/accounts/doctype/pos_invoice_merge_log/pos_invoice_merge_log.py) line ~500). If that job fails, `closing_doc.submit()` has already returned success → the dialog reports success, the closing entry is silently marked **"Failed"**, and the opening entry stays **Open** → "doesn't close, no message". (2) The existing `_validate_close_preflight` only flagged **Receivable** accounts, so other bad-account types slipped through to a cryptic GL cascade.
- **Actual root cause found (2026-06-11, via desk close):** the unmasked error was **"Row #18: Expense Account not set for the Item Bullet. Please set an Expense Account in the Items table"** ([stock_controller.check_expense_account](apps/erpnext/erpnext/controllers/stock_controller.py)). Same family as the valuation-rate issue: ERPNext defers POS stock postings to the consolidated Sales Invoice, and a stock-tracked menu item with no expense/COGS account anywhere (item / item group / brand / company `default_expense_account`) makes the stock GL posting throw and rolls the close back. **Fix:** new `set_pos_stock_expense_account` in [ury_sales_invoice.py](ury/ury/hooks/ury_sales_invoice.py) `before_submit` (alongside `allow_zero_valuation_on_pos_stock`) — for POS/consolidated stock invoices, set the company's COGS account on any item row missing an `expense_account`. `_resolve_default_cogs_account(company)` uses `Company.default_expense_account`, else the lowest-numbered enabled non-group `Cost of Goods Sold` ledger on the company (zero valuation ⇒ the posting is ₵0, so the specific COGS account doesn't affect the books — deterministic by name just keeps it predictable). Verified on CXC (no company default set) → resolves to `5110 - Food Costs - CXC`. **Needs `bench restart`** (hook-module change). If a company has NO COGS account at all, the hook is a no-op and ERPNext throws its (now visible) clear error so the admin configures one.
- **Fixes ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py) `submit_pos_closing_entry`):**
  - **Force synchronous consolidation** during close: monkeypatch `pos_invoice_merge_log.enqueue_job` to run the job inline for the duration of `submit()` (restored in `finally`). Any consolidation error now raises in-request → caught by `_wrap_close_error` → shown to the cashier. Trade-off: a very large close blocks a little longer instead of returning instantly. Plus a defensive post-submit `reload()` + `status == "Failed"` check that surfaces `error_message`.
  - **Stronger pre-flight:** `_validate_close_preflight` Check 2 now flags every bad payment account — missing / Receivable / **group** / **disabled** / **wrong-company** / nonexistent — with a precise per-Mode-of-Payment message, and logs the full account map to the Error Log when it blocks.
- **Frontend ([POSClosingDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/POSClosingDialog.tsx)):** the red error banner now uses `whitespace-pre-line` + `max-h-56 overflow-y-auto` so the multi-line per-MoP message / hint renders readably and scrolls if long.
- **Diagnosis on the dev site:** the dev branch (CXC) MoP accounts are all correct (Cash→Cash, Credit Card + 3×MOMO→Bank), pre-flight PASSES on the open shift's 5 paid invoices (no false positive), and there's no Failed closing entry / close error in the log — so the dev site can't reproduce it. On the **user's failing site** the cause is data-specific: either a MoP with a bad account on that company, or a non-MoP consolidation error (valuation rate / order_type / customer) that was dying in the ≥10-invoice background path. The new code will surface whichever it is with a clear message.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` + `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean; pre-flight no false positive on good data. **Needs `bench restart`** (whitelisted method + uses ERPNext internals). Then retry the close — the on-screen message (or a Failed closing entry's `error_message`) will name the real cause.

### 2026-06-11 — Shift-close transfer target is ALWAYS a captain (audit fix)
- **Symptom / audit:** user reported the shift-close "transfer unpaid orders" picker should show **captains**, but a captain/admin closing saw **cashiers**. Audit confirmed the 2026-06-10 design was role-aware two-way: a cashier closing → `_get_captain_users_on_branch` (captains, correct), but a captain/admin closing → `_get_cashier_users_on_branch` (cashiers). Since the user tests as Administrator (treated as a captain), they saw cashiers.
- **Decision (per user):** unpaid drafts always escalate UP to a captain/manager at close, whoever is closing. Removed the role-aware branching.
- **Backend ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py)):** `preview_pos_closing_entry` now always sets `transfer_candidates = _get_captain_users_on_branch(branch)` (minus self); `submit_pos_closing_entry` validates the target against the captain set only (`target_label = "a captain"`). Dropped the now-dead `is_captain` computation in submit; `is_captain` is still returned by preview for back-compat but no longer drives candidates.
- **Frontend ([POSClosingDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/POSClosingDialog.tsx)):** all picker copy hardcoded to "captain" — "Select a captain", "Pick a captain below…", "No captains are listed…", and the Close-button "Select a Captain" label. Removed the `is_captain`-driven ternaries.
- **Edge case:** a lone captain closing (the only captain on the branch) gets an empty candidate list → "No captains are listed… add one" and must pay/cancel their own drafts. Acceptable — the supervisor handles their own leftovers.
- **Verified:** on CXC Branch the captain set is `[derybismark025@gmail.com]` vs the old cashier set of 5; the picker now offers the captain regardless of who closes. `py_compile` + `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. **Needs `bench restart`.**

### 2026-06-11 — Daily Sales itemized view/print + per-cashier Pending KOTs count
- **Daily Sales "Itemized" toggle (default ON).** The Reports → Daily Sales tab gains an **Itemized** checkbox next to Print Report (checked by default). When on, each sale row expands to show its line items (`qty× name @ rate · amount`) both on screen and in the printout. Backend: [get_daily_sales](ury/ury_pos/api.py) now attaches `items` (`item_name, qty, rate, amount`) to each invoice via one `frappe.get_all("POS Invoice Item", filters={"parent": ["in", names]})` grouped by parent. Frontend ([Reports.tsx](pos/src/pages/Reports.tsx)): new `itemized` state + `DailySaleItem` type + `items?` on `SalesInvoice`; the on-screen table renders an indented per-invoice items box (`<React.Fragment>` per row); `handlePrint` injects an `.items-table` sub-row under each invoice when itemized. Verified the endpoint attaches items (items_attached == items_count for real invoices).
- **Pending KOTs badge is now per-cashier.** Was branch/terminal-wide (counted every cashier's held KOTs). [get_pending_kot_count](ury/ury_pos/api.py) now takes a `cashier` arg and applies `_resolve_orders_scope` (same `pi.{scope_clause}` splice as `getPosInvoice`), so the badge counts the pending KOTs of the cashier who rang the orders — matching the Pending KOTs list. Default `mine` (current user); a captain can widen via the sidebar cashier picker. Frontend: [getPendingKotCount](pos/src/lib/invoice-api.ts) gains a `cashier` param; [OrderStatusSidebar](pos/src/components/OrderStatusSidebar.tsx) passes `cashierFilter` (+ added to the `refreshPendingCount` deps). Verified the count returns the user's own scope. **Needs `bench restart`** (whitelisted method signatures changed).
- **Waiters section is now per-cashier too.** Same idea as the Pending KOTs badge: [get_waiters_with_pending_orders](ury/ury_pos/api.py) + [get_waiter_pending_order_count](ury/ury_pos/api.py) now add `AND pi.owner = %(owner)s` (= `frappe.session.user`) so the Waiters page + navbar badge only show the waiters/orders the CURRENT cashier rang. (Both use named-param SQL, so a plain `owner` clause is cleaner than `_resolve_orders_scope`'s positional splice; the Waiters page has no cashier picker, so "mine" is the only scope.) Backend-only — frontend calls unchanged. Verified: all 6 waiter drafts owned by Administrator → as Administrator count=6 / 3 waiters, as another user count=0 / 0 waiters. **Needs `bench restart`.**
- **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean; both endpoints exercised on real data.

### 2026-06-10 — Five polish fixes (Pending KOTs print-only, Return filter, Waiters nav gate, currency formatting, User-form button override)
- **Pending KOTs = print only.** In the Orders "Pending KOTs" filter the cashier should only fire the held KOTs (via Print) — no edit/payment/return. [Orders.tsx](pos/src/pages/Orders.tsx): the panel-header Edit/Cancel buttons hide when `selectedStatus === 'Pending KOTs'`; the action bar gets a dedicated Pending-KOTs branch rendering ONLY a Print button + total; `handlePrintOrder` now, after a successful print in that view, `clearSelectedOrder()` + `fetchOrders()` + bumps the badge so the order (whose KOTs are now printed) drops out of the filter. **No merging in this view either** — the "Merge Orders" button is hidden when `selectedStatus === 'Pending KOTs'`, and an effect drops out of merge mode if the cashier switches into Pending KOTs while selecting (KOTs aren't merged).
- **Return filter hidden.** Removed the "Return" entry from `EXTENDED_ORDER_STATUS_TYPES` in [order-types.ts](pos/src/data/order-types.ts) (returns are no longer used; the toggle already gated the buttons, this hides the sidebar filter too). The `OrderStatusType` union keeps "Return" for back-compat.
- **Waiters nav only when the profile uses waiters.** [Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx) now reads `posProfile.custom_use_waiter` from the store and conditionally includes the Waiters nav item (+ a separate effect that only polls the pending-order badge when waiters are on). Mirrors how the order-time waiter prompt is already gated.
- **Currency formatting (global).** [formatCurrency](pos/src/lib/utils.ts) was `${symbol} ${amount}` — no thousands separators, no decimals. Now uses `toLocaleString('en-US', {min/maxFractionDigits: 2})` → `₵ 1,234.50`. Fixes the Reports dashboard figures (uses formatCurrency, 55×) and improves every currency display app-wide. Display-only (nothing parses the result back), so safe.
- **User-form desk override clobbered standard buttons.** Opening a User in the desk showed only a "Biometrics" button and none of the standard Frappe ones (Reset Password, etc.). Root cause: [user_biometric_enrollment.js](ury/public/js/user_biometric_enrollment.js) called `frm.clear_custom_buttons('Biometrics')` — but Frappe's `clear_custom_buttons()` (form.js:1545) takes NO group arg; it clears ALL custom buttons (`this.page.clear_inner_toolbar()` + `custom_buttons = {}`), wiping the standard buttons (which Frappe/ERPNext add as custom buttons). Removed the call entirely — Frappe already clears custom buttons at every refresh start and `add_custom_button` dedupes by label, so our Biometrics buttons never duplicated without it. **Deploy note:** desk asset change → needs `bench build` (or it's served fresh in dev) + a browser hard-refresh.
- **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. (User-form fix is desk JS — verify in the browser.)
- **Follow-up — Waiters page shows only waiters WITH pending orders.** [get_waiters_with_pending_orders](ury/ury_pos/api.py) now filters out waiters whose order list is empty (`if by_waiter.get(w["name"])`), so a waiter with no pending orders no longer appears. [Waiters.tsx](pos/src/pages/Waiters.tsx) empty-state copy updated to "No pending waiter orders." Verified the endpoint returns only the 3 waiters that have orders (empties dropped).

### 2026-06-10 — Waiter system + configurable bill-reprint count + Order Status sidebar de-carded
- **Feature 1 — Waiter system.** New **URY Waiter** doctype ([ury/ury/doctype/ury_waiter/](ury/ury/doctype/ury_waiter/) — `full_name` (autoname, unique), `mobile_number`, `disabled`; JSON permissions grant URY Cashier read+create+select, Captain/Manager write). POS Profile `custom_use_waiter` (Check). When on, the React POS pops a **waiter picker** when the cashier hits the **Order button to CREATE a new order** (not on updates — confirmed with user): [OrderPanel.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderPanel.tsx) `handleSubmit(waiterOverride?)` intercepts after validations — if `useWaiter && !isUpdatingOrder && !waiter` it opens [WaiterSelectDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/WaiterSelectDialog.tsx) (search + quick-add) and stops; the dialog's onSelect re-calls `handleSubmit(picked)` passing the waiter DIRECTLY (avoids the stale-closure on `selectedWaiter`). The chosen waiter is stamped on `POS Invoice.custom_waiter` (Link) via a new `selected_waiter` kwarg on [sync_order](ury/ury/doctype/ury_order/ury_order.py) (only stamped when supplied — never cleared on update). New store field `selectedWaiter` + `setSelectedWaiter` ([pos-store.ts](pos/src/store/pos-store.ts)), cleared in resetOrderState / clearTableOrder / load-no-order. New **Waiters** nav item ([Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx)) → `/waiters` route ([App.tsx](pos/src/App.tsx)) → [Waiters.tsx](pos/src/pages/Waiters.tsx): lists all active waiters with their pending (Draft) orders + items; each order has an **Edit** button that hydrates the POS store and navigates to `/` (replicates Orders.tsx `handleEditOrder`). Backend endpoints in [ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py): `get_waiters(query)`, `create_waiter(full_name, mobile)`, `get_waiters_with_pending_orders()` (branch-scoped). Frontend client [waiter-api.ts](pos/src/lib/waiter-api.ts).
- **Feature 2 — configurable bill-reprint count.** POS Profile `custom_max_bill_prints` (Int, default 3) = TOTAL prints a CASHIER gets per bill (counts the first); captains/managers/admins unlimited (confirmed with user). New POS Invoice `custom_print_count` (Int) incremented in [qz_print_update](ury/ury/api/ury_print.py) alongside `invoice_printed` (same immediate-commit block). `getPosInvoice` SELECT returns `custom_print_count`; `getPosProfile` returns `custom_max_bill_prints`. Reprint gating in [Orders.tsx](pos/src/pages/Orders.tsx): new `canReprintNow` memo — `canReprintInvoice(user)` (captain/admin, unlimited) OR `print_count < max`. The reprint button now uses `canReprintNow` instead of the captain-only `canReprint`.
- **Waiters page refinements (same day).** (1) Narrower cards — grid changed from `lg:grid-cols-2` to `sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4` + `items-start` (cards don't stretch to the tallest in a row). (2) **Collapsible** waiter cards — header is now a toggle button with a chevron; orders body hides when collapsed (default expanded). (3) **Search** input in the header (client-side filter on name/mobile of the already-loaded list; shows a "no match" state). (4) **Navbar badge** — new lightweight `get_waiter_pending_order_count()` endpoint ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py), branch-scoped COUNT of Draft invoices with `custom_waiter` set) + `getWaiterPendingOrderCount` ([waiter-api.ts](pos/src/lib/waiter-api.ts)); [Footer.tsx](pos/src/components/Footer.tsx) polls it (30s, same as notifications, + on tab tap) and shows the count on the Waiters nav item via the existing generic `badge` render. Verified the endpoint returns the right count (4 against real test orders).
- **Waiters page polish round 2 + split auto-distribution (same day).** (a) **Split-by-value Custom mode now auto-distributes** ([PaymentDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/PaymentDialog.tsx)): new `touchedIds` set tracks which bills the cashier typed into; a `redistributeUntouched(list, touched)` helper splits the remainder (total − sum of touched) equally across the UNTOUCHED bills, cent-safe. So with 2 bills the second auto-fills the remainder; with 3+ the rest split it equally; each manual entry "locks" that bill. Wired into `setPayerAmount`/`addPayer`/`removePayer`; `touchedIds` cleared on mode-switch / enable / "Reset to even split". Removed the now-redundant per-payer "fill remaining" button (+ its `ArrowDownToLine` import). (b) **Waiters page invoices redesigned** ([Waiters.tsx](pos/src/pages/Waiters.tsx)): each pending order is now a distinct white bordered card (was a subtle `divide-y` list that read as one invoice) with an **"Invoice N of M"** badge when a waiter has >1; the whole card is clickable to edit (`cursor-pointer` + `hover:border-blue-400` + a hover "Edit" hint + a loading overlay) — the separate Edit button is gone. (c) Confirmed the Waiters list + badge only ever show **Draft** orders (`docstatus=0 AND status='Draft'` in both `get_waiters_with_pending_orders` and `get_waiter_pending_order_count`), so an order disappears once paid.
- **Feature 3 (revised) — Order Status sidebar de-carded.** User dropped the role-gating idea; instead removed the `bg-gray-50 border rounded-lg p-4` card wrapper around ONLY the Order Status list in [OrderStatusSidebar.tsx](pos/src/components/OrderStatusSidebar.tsx) (matched via its section comment so the Posting Date / Cashier cards stay) so the full sidebar width is available for longer labels ("Incoming Transfers", "Pending KOTs").
- **Schema:** the 4 new custom fields in all three sources ([custom_field.json](ury/fixtures/custom_field.json) + [setup.py](ury/setup.py) + [hooks.py](ury/hooks.py) fixtures). URY Waiter in [en.csv](ury/translations/en.csv). URY Waiter is URY-owned with JSON DocPerms, so no permissions.py change needed (cashier read/create granted in the doctype JSON).
- **Verification:** `bench --site local.16.5 migrate` clean (85 custom fields OK incl. the 4 new; 5 "failed" = pre-existing legacy mismatches). New doctype + fields confirmed; `create_waiter` / `get_waiters` / `get_waiters_with_pending_orders` return correctly; `qz_print_update` increments `custom_print_count` 0→2 over two calls. `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. **Still to do (user, browser):** (1) flip `custom_use_waiter` on a profile → ring a NEW order → waiter modal pops at the Order button → pick/quick-add → order created with the waiter; update the same order → no modal. (2) Waiters nav → see waiters + pending orders + items → Edit → opens in POS. (3) set `custom_max_bill_prints=2` → as a cashier, print a bill 2× → reprint button hides on the 3rd; as a captain → always reprints. (4) Orders sidebar → longer labels fit without the card. Needs a `bench restart` for the new whitelisted methods + doctype.

### 2026-06-10 — Cashiers transfer drafts UP to captains at close + per-terminal bill printers
- **Fix 1 — cashier can transfer unpaid drafts to a captain at shift close (was hard-blocked).** The 2026-06-05 batch made transfers captain-only: a regular cashier closing with unpaid drafts was hard-blocked ("Pay or Cancel Required") and only captains could transfer. User wants cashiers to be able to end shift AND hand pending orders to a CAPTAIN (who sees them in the Orders "Incoming Transfers" filter and approves). **Changes ([ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py)):** (1) `preview_pos_closing_entry` — `transfer_candidates` is now role-aware: a cashier gets `_get_captain_users_on_branch` (captains/managers), a captain keeps `_get_cashier_users_on_branch` (cashiers+captains, the original flow). `can_transfer` dropped the `is_captain` requirement → `int(max_transfers > 0)` so anyone can transfer when the profile allows it. `is_captain` still returned so the frontend can label the picker. (2) `submit_pos_closing_entry` — removed the non-captain hard-block; target validation is role-aware (cashier → must pick a captain; captain → any cashier/captain). Idempotency + hop-cap + Pending-transfer creation unchanged. The receiver-approval flow (`get_incoming_transfers` / `approve_transfer` / `reject_transfer`, "Incoming Transfers" sidebar filter gated on `custom_max_invoice_transfers > 0`) already shows transfers to the captain — no change needed there. **Frontend ([POSClosingDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/POSClosingDialog.tsx)):** the transfer dropdown now shows for cashiers too (driven by `can_transfer`); labels/placeholder/button text are role-aware via `preview.is_captain` ("Select a Captain" for a cashier, "Select a Cashier" for a captain); the old "only a captain can transfer" copy replaced with "transfers disabled for this profile" (only shown when `max = 0`). `tsc` + `yarn build` clean.
- **Fix 2 — per-terminal bill printers (POS Profile table instead of one printer).** POS Profile had a single `custom_bill_printer`, so two terminals sharing a profile couldn't each print to their own physical printer (the one name might not match a given till's local printer). **New child doctype [URY Terminal Bill Printer](ury/ury/doctype/ury_terminal_bill_printer/)** (istable: `terminal` Link→URY POS Terminal, `bill_printer` Link→URY Printer) + new POS Profile Table field `custom_bill_printers` (in [custom_field.json](ury/fixtures/custom_field.json) + [setup.py](ury/setup.py) + [hooks.py](ury/hooks.py) fixtures). **Resolution: backend-only** — new `_resolve_terminal_bill_printer(pos_profile_doc, terminal)` in [ury_pos/api.py](ury/ury_pos/api.py): when the requesting `terminal` has a row in `custom_bill_printers`, that row's printer wins; else falls back to the single `custom_bill_printer`. Wired at the one place `getPosProfile` returns `bill_printer` (which feeds the React POS's `printOrder` → QZ). `getPosProfile` already loads the full profile doc + receives `terminal`, so no extra query and **no frontend change** (printOrder reads the resolved `custom_bill_printer` as before). Translation added to [en.csv](ury/translations/en.csv). Verified: resolution returns the terminal-specific printer for listed terminals and falls back cleanly for unlisted/none/no-table. **Note:** the KOT fallback-to-bill-printer path in [ury_print.py](ury/ury/api/ury_print.py) still reads the profile-level `custom_bill_printer` (server-side, no terminal context) — acceptable, it's a rare last-resort and only the QZ bill print (client-side, the user's case) needed per-terminal routing.
- **Fix 3 (follow-up) — bill printed but invoice not marked printed, so the Payment button stayed hidden.** The Orders page hides Payment until `invoice_printed=1` (you print the bill, then pay). After a successful print the flag wasn't reliably landing. **Root cause:** `qz_print_update` ([ury_print.py](ury/ury/api/ury_print.py)) set `invoice_printed=1` but only committed at the END of the request — AFTER the table-free + `auto_unmerge_table_if_active` steps. If any of those errored/rolled back, the `invoice_printed` write rolled back with it (the bill had already physically printed), leaving a printed bill with no Payment button. **Fix:** mark `invoice_printed=1` and `frappe.db.commit()` it IMMEDIATELY, before the table bookkeeping (which is now best-effort in its own try/commit). **Frontend hardening ([print.ts](pos/src/lib/print.ts)):** new `markPrintedSafe()` wraps `updatePrintStatus` so a status-update hiccup can't throw out of `printOrder` after the bill already printed — `handlePrintOrder`'s optimistic `selectOrder({...invoice_printed:1})` always runs on a successful print. Verified: `qz_print_update("RS-2026-00017")` flipped `invoice_printed` 0→1 and committed. `tsc` + `yarn build` clean.
- **Verification:** `bench --site local.16.5 migrate` clean (81 custom fields OK now incl. `custom_bill_printers`; the 5 "failed" are the pre-existing legacy fieldtype mismatches). New doctype + field confirmed present; `_resolve_terminal_bill_printer` returns the right printer per terminal. `tsc --noEmit` + `yarn build` clean. **Still to do (user, browser):** (Fix 1) cashier ends shift with unpaid drafts → picks a captain → close succeeds, captain logs in → "Incoming Transfers" badge → Approve lands the order in their list; (Fix 2) add rows to POS Profile → Per-Terminal Bill Printers mapping each terminal to its printer → ring + print on terminal A vs B → each prints to its own printer. Needs a `bench restart` so workers pick up the new whitelisted code + doctype.

### 2026-06-05 — Eight-part feature batch: split-by-item, value-split receipts, returns master toggle, captain-only transfer-with-approval, PIN expiry
- **Goal:** user-requested batch of 8 changes across 4 workstreams. All build on existing features (value-only split, the Return feature, shift-close draft transfer, biometric/PIN login). Confirmed decisions up-front via two rounds of clarifying questions (see the approved plan): item split → separate invoices; value split → one invoice + 2 receipts; returns gated behind a default-OFF setting; captain-only transfer initiation; receiver approves via an Orders sidebar filter; reject routes to a captain queue.
- **Workstream 1 — Split by value or item (points 1-4, 6).**
  - The Payment dialog's **Split** tab gained a **By Value / By Item** sub-toggle ([PaymentDialog.tsx](pos/src/components/PaymentDialog.tsx)).
  - **Value split (1A):** still ONE invoice via `make_invoice` (backend unchanged). After payment, prints one **itemized receipt per payer** via a new `printSplitReceipts({orderId, posProfile, receipts})` in [print.ts](pos/src/lib/print.ts) — fetches the bill HTML once, injects a `RECEIPT k OF N · Grand Total · this portion (mode)` banner per copy, prints each via QZ. Non-QZ modes fall back to a single print (banner injection is client-side only). Right-column "Split Breakdown" relabeled Payer → **Receipt**. The per-payer array already lives in dialog state, so NO backend storage was needed.
  - **Item split (1B):** new whitelisted `split_invoice_by_item(source_invoice, bills, table)` in [ury_order.py](ury/ury/doctype/ury_order/ury_order.py). **Approach: cancel the source draft, create N brand-new submitted POS Invoices** (symmetric loop, naturally idempotent — the cancelled source is the lock against a double-split, a second call sees `docstatus != 0` and throws). Mirrors `get_order_invoice`'s field setup off the SOURCE (naming_series, is_pos, update_stock, restaurant, branch, order_type, taxes_and_charges, selling_price_list, pos_profile, custom_terminal, waiter) so a mid-shift price change can't make bills disagree. Copies POS Invoice Item rows with allocated qty (partial qty = two allocations on the same `source_row`, e.g. 3+2 of 5). KOTs are **re-pointed** to the last bill (`UPDATE tabURY KOT SET invoice=…`), NOT re-fired (food already went to the kitchen). Table freed once after the last bill. Per-bill payment uses a single `payment_mode` string → backend auto-fills the amount = computed grand total (so the frontend never has to compute per-bill tax). Allocation math extracted to a **pure `_plan_item_split(item_qtys, bills)` helper** for unit-testing (partial/over/under/leftover). New endpoint `get_order_items_for_split(invoice)` returns the draft items with row names for the allocator. Frontend: new [ItemSplitFlow.tsx](pos/src/components/ItemSplitFlow.tsx) (per-item × per-bill allocation matrix + per-bill inline customer picker reusing `searchCustomers` + per-bill payment mode + sequential settle + sequential receipt print) and [split-api.ts](pos/src/lib/split-api.ts). Mounted by PaymentDialog when Split + By Item; its Cancel returns to the value view.
